# MSC Now

_Sam's living doc. Everyone reads it, Jarvis loads it every session. Newest on top. Last edited 2026-10-19 23:48 ET._

## Top of mind (October)

1. Payouts: ACH batch broken since 10/12. Fix it and never let it happen again.
2. Margin: I want ONE answer to 'are we making money'. Ops dashboard says ~20%. Kenji's P&L says September made almost nothing. Both can't be right. (Can they?)
3. Jarvis: rethink. Cheaper, used by everyone, actually right. Proposal by end of October.
4. West: inspection backlog + resealed boxes. Dana needs help.
5. Ember Throne / Nebula Rising: we're long after the reprint news. Clear it without torching margin.
6. Northgate: biggest account, slowest payer. Don't lose them, don't fund them.

## Numbers I watch

- Packages received per day (dashboard). East ~30–60, West ~12–45. Mondays higher (Sunday mail).
- Fail rate. Normal 2–4%.
- Ops margin (avg paid, TCG + sports). Target 18–22%.
- Revenue per week. ~$350–450k lately.
- AR > 30 days.
- AI tools spend (QuickBooks).

## Standing beliefs (might be wrong)

- Distributors = volume, hobby shops = margin, live sellers = fun.
- Members are loyal if we pay fast and inspect fairly.
- Our edge is the index + speed.
- Standard partner terms are net-30. (Jarvis once told Priya 'net-90 for gold partners' — no idea where that came from.)

## Decisions log

- 2026-08-24: Northgate is now our biggest partner by volume. Sofia wants to raise their credit limit to $250k. Approved.
- 2026-08-17: DECISION: supply push approved, Aug 20 → re-evaluate Sep 21. Sam approved.
- 2026-05-14: Jarvis v0 launched (Slack bot + memory). Arjun.
- 2026-03-01: Moved TX members to West.
- 2026-02-17: Ops and finance keep separate cost numbers (avg paid vs FIFO).

### Sat Oct 17, 2026

Dashboard: East 40 / West 24 packages. Week-to-date revenue ~$307,779.

- Market page traffic up. People use our index to price. That's a moat if we keep it honest.
- Only three of us use Jarvis. Why? Need to ask people, not guess.
- Investor update due. Want to show the margin story clearly.
  - Put in leadership agenda.
- Hiring: we need a data person. Or Jarvis needs to be the data person.
- Thinking about a third warehouse in Texas. Probably not before next year.
  - Revisit next month.

If the AI is going to be in every conversation, it has to be right more often than the person it replaces. I'm worried we optimise the parts we can see and ignore the parts we can't. The real cost of the business is people time spent reconciling systems that don't talk to each other.
